  (i) Leased assets
     At inception of a contract, the Group assesses whether              (i) 租賃資產
     the contract is, or contains, a lease. This is the case if the           本集團於合約開始時評估有關合約是否
     contract conveys the right to control the use of an identified           屬租賃或包含租賃。倘合約為換取代價
     asset for a period of time in exchange for consideration.                而給予在一段時間內控制可識別資產使
     Control is conveyed where the customer has both the                      用的權利，則會出現上述情況。在客戶
     right to direct the use of the identified asset and to obtain            有權指示使用已識別資產，並有權從該
     substantially all of the economic benefits from that use.                用途獲得幾乎所有經濟利益的情況下，
                                                                              則擁有控制權。


     (i) As a lessee                                                         (i) 作為承租人
         Where the contract contains lease component(s) and                      就所有租賃而言，倘合約包含租賃
         non-lease component(s), the Group has elected not                       組成部分及非租賃組成部分，本集
         to separate non-lease components and accounts for                       團選擇不區分非租賃組成部分及將
         each lease component and any associated non-lease                       每個租賃組成部分及任何相關的非
         components as a single lease component for all leases.                  租賃組成部分列為單一的租賃組成
                                                                                 部分。


         At the lease commencement date, the Group                                於租賃開始的日期，本集團確認使
         recognises a right-of-use asset and a lease liability,                   用權資產及租賃負債，惟租期12個
         except for leases that have a short lease term of 12                     月或以下的短期租賃及低價值項目
         months or less, and leases of low-value items. When                      租賃除外。當本集團就低價值項目
         the Group enters into a lease in respect of a low-value                  訂立租賃時，本集團就每份租賃決
         item, the Group decides whether to capitalise the                        定是否將租賃資本化。倘若相關的
         lease on a lease-by-lease basis. If not capitalised, the                 租賃付款未資本化，則會於租賃期
         associated lease payments are recognised in profit or                    間按系統基準於損益中確認。
         loss on a systematic basis over the lease term.

         Where the lease is capitalised, the lease liability is                   當將租賃資本化時，租賃負債初步
         initially recognised at the present value of the lease                   以租期內應付租賃付款使用租賃
         payments payable over the lease term, discounted                         隱含之利率或（倘利率無法即時釐
         using the interest rate implicit in the lease or, if that                定）相關增量借貸利率貼現的現值
         rate cannot be readily determined, using a relevant                      確認。於初步確認後，租賃負債按
         incremental borrowing rate. After initial recognition,                   攤銷成本計量，而利息開支使用實
         the lease liability is measured at amortised cost and                    際利率法確認。計算租賃負債時並
         interest expense is recognised using the effective                       無計入並非取決於指數或利率之可
         interest method. Variable lease payments that do not                     變租賃付款，並於產生時在損益扣
         depend on an index or rate are not included in the                       除。
         measurement of the lease liability, and are charged to
         profit or loss as incurred.

                  underlying asset or to restore the underlying asset or                產所在地之估算成本，並扣減已收

                  losses (see Notes 2(g) and 2(j)(ii)).

                   Refundable rental deposits are accounted for separately            可退還租賃按金的初始公平值乃按
                   from the right-of-use assets in accordance with the                照適用於按攤銷成本計量的非股本

                   2(t)(ii) and 2(j)(i)). Any excess of the nominal value over        按金的名義價值超過初始公平值的
                   the initial fair value of the deposits is accounted for as         部分均作為額外租賃付款列賬，並
                   additional lease payments made and is included in the              計入使用權資產成本。
                   cost of right-of-use assets.

                   The lease liability is remeasured when there is a change           若指數或利率變化引致未來租賃付
                   in future lease payments arising from a change in                  款變動，若本集團預期根據剩餘價
                   an index or rate, if there is a change in the Group’s              值擔保應付的估計金額出現變化，
                   estimate of the amount expected to be payable under                或若本集團就有關是否行使購買、

          For impairment testing, assets are grouped together                      就減值測試而言，資產集合為資產之
          into the smallest group of assets that generates                         最小組別，由持續使用中產生現金流
          cash inflows from continuing use that are largely                        量，當中大部分獨立於其他資產或現

          cash-generating units (“CGU”s).                                          流量。


         The recoverable amount of an asset or CGU is the                          資產或現金產生單位的可收回金額是
         greater of its value in use and its fair value less costs of              其使用價值及其公平值減出售成本兩
         disposal. Value in use is based on the estimated future                   者中的較高者。使用價值基於估計未
         cash flows, discounted to their present value using                       來現金流，按可以反映當時市場對貨
         a pre-tax discount rate that reflects current market                      幣時間值及資產或現金產生單位特定
         assessments of the time value of money and the risks                      風險的評估的稅前貼現率，貼現至其
         specific to the asset or CGU.                                             現值。


         An impairment loss is recognised if the carrying amount                   倘一項資產或現金產生單位的賬面值
         of an asset or CGU exceeds its recoverable amount.                        超過其估計可收回金額，則會確認減
                                                                                   值虧損。
         Impairment losses are recognised in profit or loss. They
         are allocated to reduce the carrying amounts of the                       減值虧損於損益中確認。減值虧損會
         assets in the CGU on a pro rata basis.                                    予以分配，按比例減去該現金產生單
                                                                                   位中資產的賬面值。
         An impairment loss is reversed only to the extent that
         the resulting carrying amount does not exceed the                         撥回減值虧損僅以產生的賬面金額不
         carrying amount that would have been determined,                          超過倘無確認減值虧損時所釐定的賬
         net of depreciation or amortisation, if no impairment                     面金額（經扣除折舊或攤銷）為限。
         loss had been recognised.

  (k) Inventories                                                          (k) 存貨

     and comprises all costs of purchase, costs of conversion                   括所有採購成本、轉換成本及為使存貨

     present location and condition.                                            本。


     Net realisable value is the estimated selling price in the                 可變現淨值為日常業務過程中的估計售
     ordinary course of business less the estimated costs of                    價減估計完成成本及作出銷售估計所需
     completion and the estimated costs necessary to make the                   的成本。
     sale.

              of previous years. The amount of current tax payable or                 應收即期稅項金額為可反映所得稅相關

              also includes any tax arising from dividends.

              on historical cost in a foreign currency are translated at

          (v) Borrowing costs                                                    (v) 借款成本
              Borrowing costs that are directly attributable to the                與收購、建築或生產需要大量時間方可

              for its intended use or sale are capitalised as part of the          其他借款成本則在產生期間支銷。
              cost of that asset. Other borrowing costs are expensed in
              the period in which they are incurred.

  (w) Asset acquisition                                                   (w) 資產收購

     acquisitions. On an acquisition-by-acquisition basis, the                 在逐項收購的基礎上，當所收購的總資
     Group chooses to apply a simplified assessment of whether                 產的絕大部分公平值均集中於單一的可
     an acquired set of activities and assets is an asset rather               辨認資產或類似可辨認資產組別時，本
     than business acquisition, when substantially all of the fair             集團選擇採用簡化的評估方法以確定所
     value of the gross assets acquired is concentrated in a single            收購的一組活動和資產是否為資產而非
     identifiable asset or Group of similar identifiable assets.               業務收購。

     When a group of assets acquired and liabilities assumed                   當一組收購的資產和承擔的負債不構成

     allocated to the individual identifiable assets and liabilities           整體收購成本分攤至單項可辨認資產和
     based on their relative fair values at the date of acquisition.           負債。可辨認資產和負債的個別公平值
     An exception is when the sum of the individual fair values                之和不同於整體收購成本時除外。在該
     of the identifiable assets and liabilities differs from the               情況下，根據本集團的政策以成本以外

      24 Financial risk management and fair values                              24 財務風險管理及金融工具公平值
         of financial instruments
          Exposure to credit, liquidity, interest rate and currency risks        本集團在日常業務過程中面臨信貸、流動資
          arises in the normal course of the Group’s business.                   金、利率及貨幣風險。

          The Group’s exposure to these risks and the financial risk             本集團面臨的該等風險及本集團用於管理該
          management policies and practices used by the Group to                 等風險的財務風險管理政策及慣例載述如
          manage these risks are described below.                                下。

          (a) Credit risk                                                        (a) 信貸風險
              Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will default      信貸風險指對手方不履行合約責任而導
              on its contractual obligations resulting in a financial loss to      致本集團承受財務虧損的風險。本集團
              the Group. The Group’s credit risk is primarily attributable         的信貸風險主要來自貿易及其他應收款
              to trade and other receivables. The Group’s exposure to              項。本集團所承受來自現金及現金等價
              credit risk arising from cash and cash equivalents, bills            物、應收票據及衍生金融資產的信貸風
              receivables and derivative financial assets is limited because       險有限，原因是對手方為本集團管理層
              the counterparties are banks and financial institutions with         所指定具有良好信譽的銀行及金融機
              a high credit standing assigned by the management of the             構，就此而言，本集團認為信貸風險較
              Group, for which the Group considers to have low credit              低。
              risk.

              Trade receivables                                                    貿易應收款項
              The Group’s exposure to credit risk is influenced mainly by          本集團面臨的信貸風險主要受各客戶的
              the individual characteristics of each customer rather than          個別情況所影響，而非客戶經營所在的
              the industry in which the customers operate and therefore            行業，因此，重大集中信貸風險主要於
              significant concentrations of credit risk primarily arise when       本集團就個別客戶面臨重大風險時產
              the Group has significant exposure to individual customers.          生。於報告期末，貿易應收款項總額
              At the end of the reporting period, 23.47% (2023: 26.3%)             中分別有23.47%（2023年：26.3%）及
              and 68.12% (2023: 63.3%) of the total trade receivables              68.12%（2023年：63.3%）乃應收本集
              was due from the Group’s largest trade debtor and the                團最大貿易債務人及應收本集團五大貿
              Group’s five largest trade debtors, respectively.                    易債務人的賬款。

              Individual credit evaluations are performed on all customers         本集團對所有要求超過一定金額信貸的
              requiring credit over a certain amount. These evaluations            客戶進行個別信貸評估。該等評估集中
              focus on the customer’s past history of making payments              於客戶過往繳付到期款項之記錄及現時
              when due and current ability to pay, and take into account           付款的能力，並會考慮客戶的具體資料
              information specific to the customer as well as pertaining to        及與客戶營運所在地的經濟環境有關的
              the economic environment in which the customer operates.             資料。貿易應收款項一般即時及最長於
              Trade receivables are generally due immediately and up to            發票日期後90日到期。本集團一般不收
              90 days from the date of billings. Normally, the Group does          取客戶的抵押品。
              not obtain collateral from customers.

              The Group measures loss allowances for trade receivables             本集團按相等於生命週期預期信貸損失
              at an amount equal to lifetime ECLs, which is calculated             的金額計量貿易應收款項的虧損撥備，
              using a provision matrix.                                            乃採用撥備矩陣計算。
